- **review-agent-failed-to-keep-a-consiste-f13be08d3411035d**: Fixes a review agent flip-flopping between verdicts by deciding the verdict once at the end of the review from the collected findings. Use when the agent approves a PR it earlier requested changes on, or emits contradictory reviews. Triggers: verdict reversals with no code change, duplicate contradictory reviews. Not for human-overridden verdicts, or approvals earned after the code was genuinely fixed. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/review-agent-failed-to-keep-a-consiste-f13be08d3411035d))
- **review-agent-failed-to-keep-summary-an-063d97de05b43250**: Fix for review agents whose summary says one thing while the inline comments say another, for example a summary reading 'looks good, minor nits' above three critical findings. Use when summaries underplay or overplay the inline findings, reviewers distrust the verdict, or the summary was written before the findings existed. Key triggers: summary and inline severity disagreeing, verdict not matching findings. Not for summaries that are merely terse. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/review-agent-failed-to-keep-summary-an-063d97de05b43250))

- **review-artifact**: 研究、评测用的一次性人裁样本页（挑错例、验金标、扩测试集、查库里的坏数据）。整理一册书的第二步人审（切分归因、字形入库、码位、形近字对、整列抽查、收口单等）不用本 skill，一律用 guji-manager 的 `guji pipeline review-pages build &lt;页型&gt;`。出「给人裁一批样本」的交互网页（Artifact）——卡片式、手机能点、裁决用颜色标出来、**点完自动存回页面本身**，事后一条命令把结果收回来。用户说「出个审查页」「让我审一批」「做个标注页面」「把这些发给我看/裁」「人工确认一下」，或者你自己需要人来裁样本（挑错例、验金标、扩测试集、查库里的坏数据）时，都用这个 skill，不要从零手写 HTML——从零写十有八九会漏掉自存，人裁完一小时结果丢了。 ([open-guji/open-guji-cv](https://github.com/open-guji/open-guji-cv/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/review-artifact) · ★14)
